Entry 1
U: I need to look for round trips flights that depart from NYC.
A: Where are you going? And what dates will you be leaving and returning on?
U: I'll be going to Atlanta. I want to leave on the 2nd and return on March 8th.

U: Yeah, that'll work. I want to buy a ticket.
A: You want 1 Economy ticket for New York to Atlanta round trip on American Airlines. Departure is March 2nd at 11:45 am and return is March 8th at 3:25 pm. Shall I proceed with the purchase?
U: No, I actually need 4 tickets, there are four passengers.
A: You want 4 Economy tickets?
U: Yeah, that's right. What time does the departure flight arrive?
A: I've purchased the tickets. The departure flight arrives at 2:07 pm.
U: And what time does the return flight arrive?
A: 5:47 pm is the arrival time of the return flight.
U: Thanks for the help. I also need to a rent a car there.
A: What time do you want to pick it up?
U: I want to get it at during afternoon 3:30.
A: Will you be renting the car from March 2nd to March 8th?
U: No, it's just a rental from March 3rd to March 4th. And I want a compact car.
A: Would you like the car to be at Hartsfield-Jackson International Airport for pick up?
U: Yeah, that would work for me.
A: You want a Compact car at Hartsfield-Jackson International Airport for pickup on March 3rd at 3:30 pm and return on March 4th. Shall I proceed with the rental?
U: Yes, please rent it. What type of car is it?
A: I've rented the car. It is a GTI.
U: Thanks a lot for the help.
A: Do you need me for anything else?
U: No, thanks again.
A: Have a wonderful day.
